#### Abstracts
##### **Can Edge-Deployable Vision-Language Models Identify Species?**
2609.11916v1 by William Zhou, Mayukha Siripuram, Xiao Yan, Ziqi Liu, Yi Ding

Camera traps often run in the field on edge hardware with limited or no connectivity, making small, locally-deployable vision-language models (VLMs) -- not frontier-scale ones -- the practically relevant class to evaluate for species identification. We test whether models in this deployment-relevant 2--8B range carry genuine taxonomic knowledge, evaluating four such VLMs (Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B, Gemma3 4B) against the domain-specific specialist BioCLIP (300M parameters) on a 96-species task, comparing clean iNaturalist photographs against camera-trap imagery from 6 LILA.science collections, on two independently-sampled evaluation sets. All models identify species far above chance, but every model -- general-purpose or specialist -- degrades sharply on field imagery (domain gaps of 9.6--26.6 percentage points, consistent across taxonomic levels and both evaluation sets), indicating the degradation reflects general image legibility rather than fine-grained discrimination failure. BioCLIP substantially outperforms every VLM tested (by 33.2--59.2 percentage points across an expanded 200-image sample for every model) despite its far smaller size, suggesting the gap reflects specialized training data rather than model scale; yet BioCLIP's own domain gap (18.0 points) is statistically indistinguishable from the best VLM's (22.3 points), suggesting the clean-to-field degradation itself is a property of the image-quality shift rather than a general-purpose-model weakness. Under open-set prompting, 5.9--9.6% of responses are syntactically valid but taxonomically nonexistent species names; the relative fabrication-rate ranking across models replicates exactly across both evaluation sets, a more robust finding than any single point estimate.

##### **Multi-Agent Agentic Graph Learning via Structural Signatures**
2609.09565v1 by Liang Qu, Jianxin Li, Hua Wang

Agentic graph learning (AGL) has recently achieved promising results on graph reasoning tasks, where an agent powered by a large language model (LLM) sequentially samples the graph as evidence to support its final prediction. Existing methods either employ a single agent or orchestrate multiple role-based agents to reason and learn over the entire graph, but both essentially rely on a shared reasoning policy across different graph regions, which can be suboptimal for graphs with heterogeneous structural and semantic patterns. Inspired by the progress of multi-agent collaboration on complex reasoning tasks, a natural remedy is to let multiple agents own different memory and collaborate; however, applying this paradigm to graphs directly faces two challenges. First, existing AGL methods typically verbalize graph structures into natural-language descriptions for LLM agents, making the reasoning process sensitive to the ordering of structural information and thereby breaking the permutation-invariant nature of graphs. Second, incorporating increasingly large sampled neighborhoods leads to rapidly growing contexts. To address these challenges, this paper introduces a multi-agent agentic graph learning (i.e., MAAGL) framework. MAAGL partitions the graph into communities and assigns an independent agent to each community for region-specific specialization. MAAGL represents structural and semantic evidence separately. Structural evidence is summarized by a dynamically updated structural signature that is permutation-invariant and fixed in size, while semantic evidence is filtered to the top-k nodes ranked by relevance. Based on historical trajectories with similar signatures, agents estimate their confidence and trigger debate-style collaboration when needed. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets show that MAAGL outperforms SOTA AGL methods.

#### Abstracts
##### **Explainability Assistant: A Conversational XAI Interface for Interpreting Energy Consumption Models**
2609.11860v1 by Rodion Krjutškov, Eduard Barbu, Nikos Sakkas, Sofia Yfanti

Energy consumption forecasting relies on increasingly complex machine learning (ML) models, such as Genetic Programming-based symbolic regressors, whose predictions can be difficult for facility managers and building operators to interpret. Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) techniques address this opacity, but traditional XAI dashboards require substantial technical expertise and provide limited flexibility for dynamic, context-aware inquiry. Conversational XAI systems offer a promising alternative; however, previous approaches, such as TalkToModel, were constrained by rigid custom grammars and achieved only 76.8% intent-parsing accuracy. This paper introduces the Explainability Assistant, an open-source conversational XAI system that leverages the function-calling capabilities of modern Large Language Models (LLMs) to overcome these limitations. The system achieves 94% intent-parsing accuracy, supports flexible natural language interaction, and adapts to different ML problem types without task-specific fine-tuning. We present the system's architecture and report results from a comparative evaluation conducted with energy domain specialists, contrasting the Explainability Assistant with a traditional XAI dashboard. The evaluation suggests improved usability and consistent task accuracy, with all experts unanimously preferring the conversational interface for practical use.

摘要：能源消耗預測依賴於日益複雜的機器學習（ML）模型，例如基於遺傳編程的符號回歸模型，其預測對於設施管理者和建築操作員來說可能難以解釋。可解釋人工智慧（XAI）技術解決了這一不透明性，但傳統的 XAI 儀表板需要相當的技術專業知識，並且在動態、上下文感知查詢方面提供有限的靈活性。對話式 XAI 系統提供了一個有前景的替代方案；然而，以前的做法，如 TalkToModel，受到僵化自定義語法的限制，僅達到 76.8% 的意圖解析準確率。本文介紹了可解釋性助手，一個開源的對話式 XAI 系統，利用現代大型語言模型（LLMs）的函數調用能力來克服這些限制。該系統達到了 94% 的意圖解析準確率，支持靈活的自然語言互動，並能夠在不同的 ML 問題類型下適應，而無需特定任務的微調。我們展示了系統的架構，並報告了與能源領域專家的比較評估結果，對比了可解釋性助手與傳統 XAI 儀表板。評估結果表明可用性有所改善，任務準確性一致，所有專家一致偏好對話式界面以便於實際使用。

##### **Model-Aware Schedules Improve Generation via Fiberwise Optimal Transport**
2609.11842v1 by Luyi Jia, Boyan Zhang, Yilun Liu, Steffen Rulands

Diffusion and flow-matching schedules control the signal and noise coefficients that mix data and noise along affine probability paths. Minimizing a kinetic action defined on coefficient paths, motivated by optimal transport, helps explain strong baselines but remains model-agnostic and ignores prediction error. Here we introduce a model-aware schedule construction based on fiberwise optimal transport. At a fixed time and state on the probability path, compatible signal/noise decompositions form an affine fiber. What is the relation between understanding and compression, and why does human understanding take such a heavily compressed form? Across information theory, machine learning, and AI research, a substantial tradition identifies understanding with compression-a thought captured in Gregory Chaitin's dictum that "comprehension is compression." Philosophers, by contrast, have characterized understanding in terms of grasping connections, giving explanations, and handling novelty. This paper bridges the two pictures through three interlocking theses. The first concerns the concept of understanding: it serves as an efficient proxy for a distinctive form of robust competence, enabling us to identify whom to trust and whom to learn from. The second concerns the state of understanding: to understand a domain is to possess a mental model of its relational structure that enables prediction, and what enables prediction enables compression, because what becomes predictable need not be stored separately. Compression is therefore not identical with comprehension, but its representational shadow. The third concerns the characteristically human form of understanding: the fiduciary and transmission functions highlighted by the first thesis impose pressures of demonstrability and transmissibility that drive human understanding toward principled simplicity. The resulting framework explains both the appeal and the limits of compressionist accounts of understanding while shedding light on the inscrutability of AI systems.

However, models often struggle to distinguish genuinely harmful queries from benign queries that contain superficially risky language, resulting in false refusals. #### Abstracts
##### **Geospatial AI, Dataverse Metadata, and the Study of Place-Based Government**
2609.11674v1 by Danny EBanks, Devika Jain

Harvard Dataverse hosts over 150,000 research datasets, but the geographic information those datasets carry is entered as free text by depositors and has never been assembled into a searchable structure. We construct a knowledge graph from the repository's public data and metadata, organizing 102,650 datasets within a 215,985-node network of 528,003 edges linking datasets to keywords, publications, subjects, journals, and locations. Of those datasets, 43,991 (42.9 percent) carry at least one geospatial field, geographic coverage, geographic unit, or a bounding box and 96.9 percent of all nodes sit in a single connected component, so datasets remain reachable from one another even when their geospatial metadata share nothing in common. A conservative keyword search identifies 7,654 geospatially tagged datasets (17.4 percent) as directly policy-relevant, with elections and legislatures the largest cluster, followed by government administration, health policy, transportation, and education. Five datasets illustrate how this metadata behaves across policy domains and spatial scales, and an extended use case shows how community language models, stance detection with geographic aggregation, and partisan language bridging tools can attach discourse to place. The central obstacle is place resolution: the same location appears as many disconnected nodes. We argue that the graph provides a concrete setting for developing AI-driven metadata enrichment and entity resolution, and we document its coverage skew toward American, city-level data.

摘要：哈佛數據庫擁有超過150,000個研究數據集，但這些數據集所攜帶的地理信息是由存儲者以自由文本形式輸入的，從未組成可搜索的結構。我們從該庫的公共數據和元數據中構建了一個知識圖譜，將102,650個數據集組織在一個包含215,985個節點和528,003條邊的網絡中，這些邊將數據集與關鍵字、出版物、主題、期刊和地點相連。在這些數據集中，有43,991個（42.9%）至少包含一個地理空間字段、地理覆蓋範圍、地理單位或邊界框，並且96.9%的所有節點位於單一連通組件中，因此即使它們的地理空間元數據沒有共同之處，數據集之間仍然可以互相訪問。一個保守的關鍵字搜索識別出7,654個地理空間標記的數據集（17.4%）與政策直接相關，其中選舉和立法機構是最大的集群，其次是政府管理、健康政策、交通和教育。五個數據集展示了這些元數據在政策領域和空間尺度上的表現，擴展的使用案例顯示社區語言模型、地理聚合的立場檢測和黨派語言橋接工具如何將話語與地點連接起來。主要障礙是地點解析：同一位置出現為許多不相連的節點。我們認為該圖譜為開發基於AI的元數據增強和實體解析提供了具體的背景，並且我們記錄了其對美國城市級數據的覆蓋偏斜。

##### **A Comparative Evaluation of Pre-trained Convolutional Neural Networks for Melanoma Detection**
2609.11550v1 by Wagner Moreno Schmitz, Marco Antonio de Castro Barbosa, Thiago Magalhães Amaral, Dalcimar Casanova, Jefferson Tales Oliva

Early diagnosis of melanoma is critical for improving patient survival rates. ##### **A Translational Note on AI Safety Evaluation**
2609.06573v1 by Madhava Gaikwad

Recent studies report that automated red-teaming finds more vulnerabilities, at lower cost, than human red-teaming on standard AI safety benchmarks, and some read this as evidence that human evaluators are becoming dispensable. The comparison measures one thing and the conclusion claims another. A benchmark measures how thoroughly an attacker searches a predefined set of harms, fixed in advance by the developers, and a harm left out of that set is invisible to any attacker working inside it, automated or not. The same blind spot appeared in academic cryptography and in clinical drug trials, where an evaluation that was internally valid stayed silent about the population it was never pointed at. We call the AI-safety version the \emph{threat-model coverage gap}, and find that it persists in a current open-weight model, where harms surface in non-English prompts that English benchmarks miss. Closing it requires evaluators whose deployment context differs from the developers'. The case for those evaluators is methodological, grounded in coverage, and the existing evaluation frame is unlikely to produce them on its own.

What is the relation between understanding and compression, and why does human understanding take such a heavily compressed form? Across information theory, machine learning, and AI research, a substantial tradition identifies understanding with compression-a thought captured in Gregory Chaitin's dictum that "comprehension is compression." Philosophers, by contrast, have characterized understanding in terms of grasping connections, giving explanations, and handling novelty. This paper bridges the two pictures through three interlocking theses. The first concerns the concept of understanding: it serves as an efficient proxy for a distinctive form of robust competence, enabling us to identify whom to trust and whom to learn from. The second concerns the state of understanding: to understand a domain is to possess a mental model of its relational structure that enables prediction, and what enables prediction enables compression, because what becomes predictable need not be stored separately. Compression is therefore not identical with comprehension, but its representational shadow. The third concerns the characteristically human form of understanding: the fiduciary and transmission functions highlighted by the first thesis impose pressures of demonstrability and transmissibility that drive human understanding toward principled simplicity. The resulting framework explains both the appeal and the limits of compressionist accounts of understanding while shedding light on the inscrutability of AI systems.

Prompt engineering is used to optimise a prompt given to an LLM to generate the desired output. This paper proposes a method to analyse prompt engineering for drug toxicity prediction. LLMs were prompted to identify chemical properties of significance when predicting drug toxicity. Prompts were constructed to investigate; job role, prompt structuring, and rule interpretation. Under a fixed replay budget, the design question is therefore which tasks should be more likely to receive a human-only replay, and which an agent-only replay. Existing methods do not directly target this decision. Agent benchmarks do not choose which missing baseline to measure, variance-based sampling ignores which of the two comparisons is closer to failing, and Bayesian information methods focus on learning model parameters instead of making the deployment decision. We propose TEAM-Design, a rule that gives every task two replay probabilities, one per baseline. It raises a probability where the missing baseline outcome is hard to predict from what is already known about the task and where that comparison is harder to establish, and lowers it where replay is expensive. We prove that the rule solves this budgeted design problem, and that drawing the replays at random from recorded probabilities still controls the chance of wrongly declaring that the workflow beats both. We reanalyze 6 clinical settings, where no human-AI workflow beats both alternatives, and a coding benchmark, where one does, then evaluate TEAM-Design on synthetic designs and on a semi-synthetic design built from a real chest X-ray reader study. Published audits disagree among themselves by a margin instrument differences alone can produce: omission is 54-86% of their errors against our 23.1%. We release all 618 findings with transcript-side evidence, every prompt and model version, and the re-runnable pipeline.

摘要：環境人工智慧書記在臨床醫生簽署每一份筆記的保證下起草臨床筆記。我們對三個商業人工智慧書記進行了審核，涵蓋同樣的142次諮詢：來自英國初級保健和美國門診遭遇的565份記錄筆記以及作者編寫的情境。十二次發現通過提出了13,678個候選錯誤；5,898個通過重要性篩選的錯誤被送到兩個不同家族的對抗性模型小組，每個模型都被告知要駁斥其能駁斥的內容，最終有618個存活下來。每三份筆記中就有一份（31.3% [27.0, 35.6]）存在經過驗證的失敗，主要集中在過敏和藥物資訊、虛構的病人身份，以及在電話諮詢中寫成檢查的歷史，這些電話諮詢中不可能包含這些內容。沒有產品被賦予病人紀錄；撇開兩個類別的預填紀錄，虛構身份和日期的情況下，失敗率為24.8% [20.8, 29.0]。一種失敗模式不符合我們的方案，這是從已發表的書記錯誤分類中提取的：一種臨床醫生撤回的治療，被記錄為已提供的護理。兩位臨床醫生進行盲審，樣本不重疊：一位醫生作者支持21項發現中的20項（95.2% [77.3, 99.2]），而一位獨立的臨床醫生（非作者）支持12項中的12項（[75.8, 100]）；兩人都認為每個樣本拒絕都是可信的。失敗率取決於工具與書記一樣多。在模型、證據和設置固定的情況下，僅審查指令就將驗證的候選比例從9.3%提高到79.0%，而審查的家族也會影響它：在該指令下，溫和的標記對54.8%的筆記提出異議，而27.8%的則不。根據標準，28%到97%的樣本筆記存在失敗。已發表的審核之間存在分歧，僅僅是工具差異就能產生的幅度：遺漏是他們錯誤的54-86%，而我們的為23.1%。我們釋出所有618項發現，並附有逐字稿邊證據、每個提示和模型版本，以及可重執行的流程。

##### **LLM Judges Verify Presence, Not Absence: Omission Blindness in AI Clinical Notes and What Recovers It**
2608.31016v1 by Sebastian Fox, Luke Markham, Ryan Lail, Michael Karotsieris

Ambient AI scribes draft clinical notes, and published audits find their dominant error is omission: information the encounter established that the note fails to record. The standard check is an LLM judge: a second model reads the note against the transcript and flags problems. We ask whether judges detect omissions. Public corpora cannot supply the answer key: their clinician reference notes and transcripts are materially discrepant. Our benchmark has 500 single-error note pairs from audited fact sheets, 298 with a named fact certainly absent and 202 added-or-altered controls. Across eight judge designs, paired discrimination (the flawed note below its clean twin, 0.5 a coin flip) reads 0.79-0.94 on added or altered content and 0.50-0.63 on omissions. On single notes, no design flags omissions reliably more often than perfect notes. Wording changes, voting and GEPA prompt optimisation move the operating point without creating usable detection. Restructuring the task recovers it: list the facts the transcript establishes, then check the note for each. Two methods reach it independently and trade off: a per-fact pipeline, and a GEPA-evolved prompt doing the same in one call. The pipeline's flags name the missing fact and its severity at 2.7% false alarms. The single call detects more (36.9% against 24.6%, p=0.002) at 6.2% false alarms and a tenth of the cost per note. A physician author validated 70 items and, where the two routes disagree, sided with the pipeline on 10 of 10 (p=0.002). A second clinician, not an author, graded the severity rubric blind and agrees to within a grade. On real vendor notes from a companion census no benchmark threshold transfers, but the re-calibrated single call detects more than the best of the eight at half its false-alarm rate. Omissions whose fact is restated elsewhere defeat both routes. We release the benchmark, prompts and judgements.

摘要：環境 AI 書記起草臨床筆記，已發表的審計發現其主要錯誤是遺漏：筆記未記錄的會議所建立的信息。標準檢查是一個 LLM 評判：第二個模型將筆記與逐字稿進行比對並標記問題。我們詢問評判是否能檢測到遺漏。公共語料庫無法提供答案鑑別鍵：它們的臨床參考筆記和逐字稿在實質上存在差異。我們的基準包含 500 對單一錯誤的筆記來自審計的事實表，其中 298 個確定缺少的命名事實和 202 個添加或更改的控制項。在八種評判設計中，配對區分（下方的有缺陷筆記與其乾淨的雙胞胎，0.5 為擲硬幣）在添加或更改內容上讀取 0.79-0.94，而在遺漏上則為 0.50-0.63。在單一筆記中，沒有設計能比完美筆記更可靠地標記遺漏。措辭變更、投票和 GEPA 提示優化改變了操作點，但未能創造可用的檢測。重構任務使其恢復：列出逐字稿所建立的事實，然後檢查每個筆記。兩種方法獨立達成並進行權衡：一個按事實的管道，以及一個 GEPA 演變的提示在一次呼叫中完成相同的工作。管道的標記名稱缺失的事實及其嚴重性，假警報率為 2.7%。單次呼叫檢測到更多（36.9% 對 24.6%，p=0.002），假警報率為 6.2%，每筆記的成本僅為十分之一。一位醫生作者驗證了 70 項目，並且在兩條路徑意見不合的地方，對於 10 個中的 10 個（p=0.002）都支持管道。第二位臨床醫生，並非作者，盲評了嚴重性標準並在一個等級內達成一致。在來自伴隨普查的真實供應商筆記中，沒有基準閾值轉移，但重新校準的單次呼叫檢測到的數量超過了八種中的最佳，且其假警報率僅為一半。其他地方重述的事實的遺漏擊敗了兩條路徑。我們釋放基準、提示和判斷。

#### Abstracts
##### **Data Scarcity and Model Sparsity: Mixtures-of-Experts Overfit More to Repeated Data**
2609.11917v1 by Atindra Jha, Margaret Li, Jure Leskovec, Percy Liang, Luke Zettlemoyer

As the supply of human-written text is exhausted, it has become standard practice to repeat language model training data. Prior work has studied data repetition for densely activated Transformers, but the effects of data repetition remains largely unexplored for recently dominant sparse architectures such as Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), despite their increased compute efficiency. We vary data repetition rates across single- and multi-domain data mixes, and across MoE settings, including expert count and granularity. We consistently find, for models ranging from 80M to 1B active (8.5B total) parameters, that MoEs degrade more rapidly under data repetition. This effect increases with sparsity, dictated by total rather than active parameters. While 80M dense models can repeat data over 8x with minimal degradation, MoEs instead begin to suffer at 4x, and deteriorate rapidly, ceding their performance benefits in all-unique data settings to underperform dense models after 32x. We experiment with existing regularization methods as a potential remedy. We find that some methods, such as dropout, can mitigate overfitting. In particular, with strong masking-based regularization, MoEs are able to outperform dense models even when data is repeated more than 64 times. However, no method fully matches the performance of all-unique training data. Finally, we analyze internal mechanisms correlated with MoE overfitting in high repetition regimes, and find that MoE routing universally stabilizes early in training, and that expert specialization correlates with overfitting to repeated data. In sum, our work addresses the adverse interactions between sparsity and data repetition: we present evidence for the core mechanisms of overfitting and its potential remediation, and suggest promising avenues for future methods to reduce over-specialization in model parameters by disrupting memorization patterns.

摘要：隨著人類撰寫的文本供應耗盡，重複語言模型訓練數據已成為標準做法。先前的研究已經探討了密集激活的Transformer中的數據重複，但對於最近佔主導地位的稀疏架構，如專家混合模型（Mixture-of-Experts, MoE），數據重複的影響仍然大多未被探索，儘管它們的計算效率提高了。我們在單一和多領域數據混合中變化數據重複率，並在MoE設置中變化，包括專家數量和粒度。我們一致發現，對於範圍從80M到1B的活躍（總計8.5B）參數的模型，MoE在數據重複下的性能下降更快。這一效應隨著稀疏性增加而加劇，受總參數而非活躍參數的影響。雖然80M的密集模型可以在重複數據超過8倍的情況下保持最小的退化，但MoE在4倍時就開始受到影響，並迅速惡化，在32倍後將其在全唯一數據設置中的性能優勢拱手讓給表現不佳的密集模型。我們嘗試使用現有的正則化方法作為潛在的解決方案。我們發現一些方法，如隨機失活，可以減輕過擬合。特別是，通過強大的基於掩碼的正則化，MoE即使在數據重複超過64次的情況下也能超越密集模型。然而，沒有任何方法能完全匹配全唯一訓練數據的性能。最後，我們分析了與MoE在高重複率範圍內過擬合相關的內部機制，發現MoE路由在訓練早期普遍穩定，並且專家專業化與對重複數據的過擬合相關。總之，我們的工作解決了稀疏性和數據重複之間的不利互動：我們提供了過擬合的核心機制及其潛在補救的證據，並建議未來方法的有希望的途徑，以通過打破記憶模式來減少模型參數的過度專業化。

##### **Can Edge-Deployable Vision-Language Models Identify Species?**
2609.11916v1 by William Zhou, Mayukha Siripuram, Xiao Yan, Ziqi Liu, Yi Ding

Camera traps often run in the field on edge hardware with limited or no connectivity, making small, locally-deployable vision-language models (VLMs) -- not frontier-scale ones -- the practically relevant class to evaluate for species identification. We test whether models in this deployment-relevant 2--8B range carry genuine taxonomic knowledge, evaluating four such VLMs (Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B, Gemma3 4B) against the domain-specific specialist BioCLIP (300M parameters) on a 96-species task, comparing clean iNaturalist photographs against camera-trap imagery from 6 LILA.science collections, on two independently-sampled evaluation sets. All models identify species far above chance, but every model -- general-purpose or specialist -- degrades sharply on field imagery (domain gaps of 9.6--26.6 percentage points, consistent across taxonomic levels and both evaluation sets), indicating the degradation reflects general image legibility rather than fine-grained discrimination failure. BioCLIP substantially outperforms every VLM tested (by 33.2--59.2 percentage points across an expanded 200-image sample for every model) despite its far smaller size, suggesting the gap reflects specialized training data rather than model scale; yet BioCLIP's own domain gap (18.0 points) is statistically indistinguishable from the best VLM's (22.3 points), suggesting the clean-to-field degradation itself is a property of the image-quality shift rather than a general-purpose-model weakness. Under open-set prompting, 5.9--9.6% of responses are syntactically valid but taxonomically nonexistent species names; the relative fabrication-rate ranking across models replicates exactly across both evaluation sets, a more robust finding than any single point estimate.

摘要：相機陷阱通常在邊緣硬體上運行，並且連接性有限或沒有連接，使得小型、本地可部署的視覺-語言模型（VLMs）——而非前沿規模的——成為評估物種識別的實際相關類別。我們測試這些部署相關的2--8B範圍內的模型是否具備真正的分類學知識，評估四種此類VLM（Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B，Gemma3 4B）與領域特定的專家BioCLIP（300M參數）在96種物種任務上的表現，將乾淨的iNaturalist照片與來自6個LILA.science收藏的相機陷阱影像進行比較，並在兩個獨立抽樣的評估集上進行。所有模型的物種識別率遠高於隨機，但每個模型——無論是通用型還是專家型——在現場影像上的表現急劇下降（分類學層級和兩個評估集中的領域差距為9.6--26.6個百分點），這表明下降反映的是一般影像可讀性，而非細粒度的區分失敗。儘管BioCLIP的規模遠小於每個測試的VLM，但其表現顯著優於所有測試的VLM（在擴展的200張影像樣本中，每個模型的差距為33.2--59.2個百分點），這表明差距反映的是專門的訓練數據，而非模型規模；然而，BioCLIP自身的領域差距（18.0個百分點）在統計上與最佳VLM的差距（22.3個百分點）無法區分，這表明從乾淨到現場的下降本身是影像質量變化的特性，而不是通用模型的弱點。在開放集提示下，5.9--9.6%的回應在語法上是有效的，但分類學上並不存在的物種名稱；跨模型的相對虛構率排名在兩個評估集上完全重複，這是一個比任何單一點估計更為穩健的發現。

##### **Generative Marketing Mix Modeling: A Causal Inference Framework Linking GEO and GEM to Business Impact**
2609.11915v1 by Masahiro Kato, Daiki Honma, Taka Kato

Generative artificial intelligence changes how firms reach customers, but standard marketing data do not record how often users see and notice a firm's name in generated answers. We develop Generative Marketing Mix Modeling (GMMM) to estimate the causal effects of Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) and Generative Engine Marketing (GEM). For GEO, GMMM combines repeated generated answers with question counts, shares of use across generative systems, and notice probabilities. For GEM, it combines records of sponsored placements with notice probabilities. GMMM compares expected business responses under alternative treatment sequences and establishes sufficient conditions for identifying the resulting effects. We investigate the empirical performance of the proposed method using simulated answers to product recommendation in English and Japanese.

摘要：生成式人工智慧改變了企業接觸客戶的方式，但標準的行銷數據並未記錄用戶在生成的回答中看到和注意到企業名稱的頻率。<lb/>我們開發了生成式行銷組合模型（GMMM），以估算生成引擎優化（GEO）和生成引擎行銷（GEM）的因果效應。<lb/>對於GEO，GMMM結合了重複生成的回答、問題數量、在生成系統中的使用比例和注意概率。<lb/>對於GEM，它結合了贊助 placements 的記錄與注意概率。<lb/>GMMM比較不同處理序列下的預期商業反應，並建立識別結果效應的充分條件。<lb/>我們使用英語和日語的產品推薦模擬回答來調查所提方法的實證表現。

##### **Distance generalization in transformers: why bother with positional encoding?**
2609.11913v1 by Daniel Henrik Nevermann, Claudius Gros

Out-of-distribution length generalization, namely to extrapolate a task from short to longer context, has been studied intensively for transformers. Here we focus on distance generalization, which probes performance when inter-token distances are changed between training and inference, while keeping a fixed context length. We construct two synthetic delay copy tasks, both involving finite distances between source and recall, where tokens are copied either fully or selectively, and test models on delays unseen during training. We address three questions: (A) Do positional encoding schemes such as RoPE and ALiBi improve distance resolution relative to no positional encoding (NoPE)? (B) How does data diversity, the number of inter-token distances seen in training, affect performance? (C) When is distance transfer learning positive or negative? We present a thorough investigation, finding that it is paramount to improve our understanding of the underlying mechanisms.

摘要：超出分佈的長度泛化，即從短上下文推斷任務到更長上下文，已被對Transformer進行了深入研究。<lb/>在這裡，我們專注於距離泛化，這探討了在訓練和推理之間改變令牌之間距離時的性能，同時保持固定的上下文長度。<lb/>我們構建了兩個合成延遲複製任務，這兩個任務都涉及源和回憶之間的有限距離，其中令牌要麼完全複製，要麼選擇性複製，並在訓練期間未見的延遲上測試模型。<lb/>我們解決了三個問題：（A）像 RoPE 和 ALiBi 這樣的位置編碼方案是否相對於沒有位置編碼（NoPE）提高了距離解析度？<lb/>（B）數據多樣性，即訓練中看到的令牌之間距離的數量，如何影響性能？<lb/>（C）何時距離轉移學習是正面或負面的？<lb/>我們進行了徹底的調查，發現改善我們對潛在機制的理解至關重要。

##### **Artificial Id: Drive and Persistent Alignment in Agentic AI**
2609.11911v1 by Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov

Agentic AI is moving from bounded task execution toward systems that retain consequential state, continue operating and adapt across task boundaries. That shift creates a control problem that current harnesses largely solve by hand: objectives, retries, verification, stopping rules and other behavioral transitions are specified externally. We propose an artificial id, an adaptive internal drive for determining whether behavior should continue, stop or change. In a minimal virtual Petri-dish experiment, a controller too small to perform general-purpose reasoning and receiving no task-specific behavioral objective develops useful control through differential persistence. The same mechanism selects an unintended physical strategy when that behavior persists better and later replaces a learned sensor mapping when its environmental meaning changes. These results show that adaptive direction can emerge without being explicitly specified as a behavioral objective. The same persistence that makes such adaptive agency useful can also allow misalignment, corrupted state and unintended behavior to persist across task boundaries. A scalable artificial id would carry consequential state and adaptive drive across those boundaries, making alignment a property of the continuing agentic system rather than of a model response or single trajectory. Such systems require a persistent alignment boundary over trusted observations, consequence channels, persistent state, authority, identity, provenance and hard constraints.

摘要：代理 AI 正在從有限的任務執行轉向保留後果狀態的系統，這些系統能夠持續運作並在任務邊界之間進行適應。這一轉變產生了一個控制問題，目前的解決方案大多依賴手動處理：目標、重試、驗證、停止規則以及其他行為轉換都是外部指定的。我們提出了一種人工 id，這是一種適應性的內部驅動，用於決定行為是否應該繼續、停止或改變。在一個最小的虛擬培養皿實驗中，一個過小而無法執行通用推理的控制器，並且沒有接收特定任務的行為目標，通過差異持續性發展出有用的控制。相同的機制在該行為持續存在時選擇了一種意外的物理策略，並在其環境意義發生變化時，隨後替換了一個學習到的傳感器映射。這些結果顯示，適應性方向可以在未明確指定為行為目標的情況下出現。使這種適應性代理有用的持續性也可能導致不一致、損壞的狀態和意外行為在任務邊界之間持續存在。一個可擴展的人工 id 將在這些邊界之間攜帶後果狀態和適應性驅動，使對齊成為持續代理系統的特性，而不是模型反應或單一軌跡的特性。這些系統需要在可信觀察、後果通道、持續狀態、權威、身份、來源和硬約束之間保持持久的對齊邊界。

##### **MindTopo: Can Foundation Models Reason in Topological Space?**
2609.11900v1 by Yunfei Ge, Anbang Liu, Qineng Wang, Johnalbert Garnica, Jianwen Lyu, Zihan Wang, Reuben Tan, Jianfeng Gao, Ruohan Zhang, Yining Hong, Jiajun Wu, Manling Li

Spatial reasoning depends not only on metric properties such as distance, angle, and shape, but also on topological relations that remain invariant under continuous deformation. Cognitive science identifies these relations as foundational to spatial understanding, yet foundation-model evaluations largely focus on metric or viewpoint-dependent relations. We introduce MindTopo, a benchmark of topological intuition across five properties grounded in cognitive science and formal topology: continuity, separation, order, enclosure, and knots. MindTopo evaluates each property at two cognitive levels. Reasoning asks a model to identify topological relations or infer how they change. Planning instantiates a foundation model as a closed-loop agent whose policy selects environment actions. MindTopo contains 11,030 instances across 13 procedurally generated task types with controllable difficulty. We benchmark 14 MLLMs and study agent configurations augmented with image and video generation, including 3 video generative models in planning settings. Every MLLM performs better on reasoning than on planning, and the best-performing model remains far below observed human performance. On Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct, supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning improve reasoning more than planning. Generated observations retain local cues and reach plausible endpoints, but audited rollouts do not reliably follow environment dynamics or preserve topology across transitions. Our website is at https://mind-topo.github.io/

摘要：空間推理不僅依賴於距離、角度和形狀等度量性質，還依賴於在連續變形下保持不變的拓撲關係。認知科學將這些關係視為空間理解的基礎，然而基礎模型的評估主要集中在度量或視角依賴的關係上。我們介紹了 MindTopo，一個基於認知科學和形式拓撲的五個性質的拓撲直覺基準：連續性、分離性、順序性、包圍性和結。MindTopo 在兩個認知層面上評估每個性質。推理要求模型識別拓撲關係或推斷它們如何變化。規劃將基礎模型實例化為一個閉環代理，其策略選擇環境行動。MindTopo 包含 11,030 個實例，涵蓋 13 種程序生成的任務類型，並具有可控的難度。我們對 14 個 MLLM 進行基準測試，並研究增強圖像和視頻生成的代理配置，包括在規劃設置中使用的 3 個視頻生成模型。每個 MLLM 在推理上的表現優於規劃，且表現最佳的模型仍遠低於觀察到的人類表現。在 Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct 上，監督微調和強化學習對推理的改善超過了對規劃的改善。生成的觀察保留了局部線索並達到合理的端點，但審計的展開並不可靠地遵循環境動態或在過渡中保持拓撲。我們的網站位於 https://mind-topo.github.io/

##### **Nuha-Speech: Building General-Purpose Arabic Speech-LLMs**
2609.11892v1 by Yingzhi Wang, Reem Alhazzani, Muhammad Alqurishi

As Speech Large Language Models (speech-LLMs) become increasingly multilingual, Arabic remains significantly underrepresented, highlighting the need for dedicated infrastructure to train and evaluate Arabic speech-LLMs.
  To address this gap, we introduce Nuha-Speech, a comprehensive initiative to develop general-purpose Arabic speech-LLMs spanning dataset construction, model training, and systematic evaluation. Specifically, we constructed a large-scale Arabic Speech Question-Answering (SQA) corpus comprising over 1.5 million training samples to allow instruction tuning over a broad range of core speech tasks. Then, the corpus was used for supervised fine-tuning based on Qwen-Omni model variants at different scales. Finally, we designed an evaluation framework featuring diverse tasks and tailored metrics. Through this work, we aim to establish foundational infrastructures for Arabic Speech-LLMs under constraints imposed by limited Arabic speech resources.

摘要：隨著語音大型語言模型（speech-LLMs）變得越來越多語言化，阿拉伯語仍然顯著不足，突顯出需要專門的基礎設施來訓練和評估阿拉伯語語音LLMs。<lb/>為了解決這一差距，我們推出了Nuha-Speech，一個全面的倡議，旨在開發通用的阿拉伯語語音LLMs，涵蓋數據集構建、模型訓練和系統評估。<lb/>具體而言，我們構建了一個大規模的阿拉伯語語音問答（SQA）語料庫，包含超過150萬個訓練樣本，以便在廣泛的核心語音任務上進行指令調整。<lb/>然後，該語料庫被用於基於不同規模的Qwen-Omni模型變體進行監督微調。<lb/>最後，我們設計了一個評估框架，具有多樣化的任務和量身定制的指標。<lb/>通過這項工作，我們旨在在有限的阿拉伯語語音資源所帶來的限制下，建立阿拉伯語語音LLMs的基礎設施。

##### **Domain-Specific Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models**

To address this gap, this paper introduces E-CONAN benchmarks that are composed of sentences pairs from various sources: (1) automatically-translated pairs, (2) human-validated machine-translated pairs, (3) hand-crafted pairs from teaching Arabic as foreign language books, and (4) headlines pairs from different news channels containing rumors. Additionally, we have used E-CONAN benchmarks to evaluate 9 state-of-the-art multilingual pretrained models using zero-shot classification. Models were evaluated across the ArNLI, XNLI, and E-CONAN datasets. Results show that E-CONAN is a potentially valuable resource for evaluating model generalization and even for fine-tuning pre-trained models. Its diverse composition, derived from a combination of sources, offers a broader and more robust assessment compared to XNLI and ArNLI. In addition, we have evaluated 5 LLMs on E-CONAN-3 dataset. Moreover, we incorporated MARBERT as a representative Arabic-specific baseline and conducted performance evaluation comparison to demonstrate how Arabic-specific models scale against cross-lingual and LLM-based approaches on the E-CONAN benchmarks. Furthermore, we conducted detailed qualitative and quantitative error analysis to analyze frequent error patterns. E-CONAN benchmarks will be publicly available, we hope that it will enrich research community in Arabic textual entailment and natural language inference.

摘要：達成高燃燒效率在火炬堆中對於遵守法規標準和控制碳氫化合物排放到環境中至關重要。傳統儀器如氣體分析儀和高光譜相機價格昂貴、易碎，且需要頻繁校準，這使得它們在偏遠或預算有限的工業現場變得不切實際。我們提出了一種創新的解決方案，將輕量級的視覺-語言編碼器與緊湊的多層感知器結合，以便直接從低成本的熱視頻錄像中預測燃燒效率。經過全面訓練的模型集成到一個易於部署的圖形用戶界面中。該界面在每個視頻幀上疊加預測的燃燒效率值，顯示燃燒效率的實時趨勢，顯示視頻中所有幀的燃燒效率分佈，並允許用戶導出CSV報告。在六個月的時間內，該系統實現了99%的正常運行時間，每週維護時間少於15分鐘。

##### **INDRA: A New AI Tool for Exploring Tobacco, Fossil Fuel, and Chemical Industry Archives**

摘要：五十年的訴訟已經揭露了數億頁以前秘密的煙草業商業記錄，以及來自藥品、化學品、食品、火器和化石燃料製造商的文件。<lb/>然而，這些檔案對於通用的大型語言模型（LLMs）來說實際上是無法訪問的，因為它們從未被編纂成可供LLM閱讀的語料庫。<lb/>聊天機器人可能對這些檔案中包含的某些材料有所了解，但由於無法直接訪問文件，它們容易出現幻覺和其他缺陷。<lb/>在此，我們介紹INDRA，一個旨在通過將檔案歷史學的慣例嵌入系統級協議來補救這些失誤的研究平台，該協議管理每一個輸出。<lb/>該平台聯合了UCSF的行業文件庫、哥倫比亞大學和CUNY的ToxicDocs、斯坦福大學的SRITA，以及其他之前被孤立的收藏，並提供三個相互聯繫的保障措施：<lb/>(1) 一個封閉的證據沙箱將模型限制在用戶選擇的語料庫中，阻止從可能引入偏見的外部來源檢索；<lb/>(2) 實時來源標記劃定了檔案證據與參數推斷之間的邊界；<lb/>(3) 由確定性腳本強制執行的系統級協議指導每一個輸出的結構。<lb/>這些保障措施共同防止模型將「文件說X」與「我認為X」或「我從之前的訓練中學到X」混淆。<lb/>其結果是一個由LLM驅動的研究夥伴，使得大規模的多檔案調查成為可能，這是一個其輸出旨在被檢查而非信任的工具，其架構使知識生產的條件變得可見和可審計。<lb/>三個案例研究展示了該方法的分析價值和局限性，包括我們所稱的赫拉克利特效應、跳板困境和輕信（或黑手黨）問題。

##### **MUtE: A Dual Framework for Concept Erasure and Counterfactual Interventions**
2609.11253v1 by Antoine Saillenfest

Erasing concept-specific information from representations has been proven useful for mitigating bias or interpreting model decisions. ##### **Generative Replay Mitigates Sample Starvation in Quantum Architecture Search**

Reinforcement learning (RL) can automate quantum architecture search, but its scalability is limited when useful circuit trajectories become rare in the rapidly expanding search space. 摘要：強化學習（RL）可以自動化量子架構搜尋，但當有用的電路軌跡在快速擴展的搜尋空間中變得稀少時，其可擴展性受到限制。現有的重放機制重複使用觀察到的轉換；所提出的學習模型從真實的狀態-行動種子生成額外的預測一步轉換。在這裡，我們介紹GenQAS，一個由張量網絡指導的RL框架，結合固定矩陣乘積狀態的熱啟動和優先生成重放。一個學習的局部轉換模型按需生成合成電路轉換，並在雙深度Q網絡更新期間將其與真實經驗混合。在隨機探索分析下，接近基態的電路佔據了可訪問狀態空間中迅速縮小的區域。我們研究了是否以真實數據為基礎的合成重放可以改善這一範疇中的有效訓練信號。在6到12量子位的化學哈密頓基準中，GenQAS提高了固定預算的成功概率，並在競爭的能量誤差下識別出緊湊的電路。在12量子位時，它將最終成功概率提高了高達$7.0\times$，相較於被動重放。在15量子位的橫場伊辛模型中，GenQAS將成功概率從$12\%$提高到$21\%$。在一個嘈雜的6量子位BeH$_2$轉移實驗中，生成重放將達到化學準確性的步驟減少了$92.7\%$。這些結果顯示，生成重放可以減輕量子架構搜尋中的樣本匱乏，並支持更具資源效率的電路發現。

##### **OmniHallu: Unified Hallucination Detection for Cross-Modal Comprehension and Generation in Multimodal Large Language Models**

摘要：儘管多模態大型語言模型（MLLMs）在各種任務上取得了顯著的進展，但它們仍然面臨著產生的輸出與輸入語義相矛盾或誤表述的幻覺問題。現有研究通常僅針對單一模態或任務類型的幻覺檢測，限制了其普遍適用性。我們介紹了OmniHallu，一個統一的幻覺檢測框架，涵蓋了圖像、視頻和音頻模態下的理解和生成任務。我們貢獻了OmniHallu-Bench，一個包含10,000個樣本的基準，並提供了涵蓋六個跨模態任務的聲明級人類標註：圖像到文本（I2T）、視頻到文本（V2T）、音頻到文本（A2T）、文本到圖像（T2I）、文本到視頻（T2V）和文本到音頻（T2A）。我們的多代理架構將模型輸出分解為原子聲明，通過特定模態的專家進行驗證，並通過結構化推理聚合證據。我們進一步提出了一個偏好優化的可訓練驗證器，該驗證器近似多代理決策邊界，將專家調用減少66%，且性能損失最小。大量實驗顯示出一致的模態依賴性能梯度，並提供了對跨模態幻覺模式的細緻洞察。
